### [95] [Information Lattice Learning as Probabilistic Graphical Model Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19366)
*Haizi Yu,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了信息格学习（ILL）在概率质量函数场景下与概率图模型（PGM）的深层联系：ILL学习的规则集对应于可解释抽象变量上的边际约束，其最大无知重建等价于最大熵原理下的对数线性因子图，但ILL的信息格结构本身并非贝叶斯网络，而是对商变量进行约束的因子图结构学习框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立ILL与概率图模型之间的正式联系，阐明ILL学习到的概率规则集如何从PGM视角理解，从而为ILL提供更坚实的理论基础，并探索其与最大熵模型、因子图等经典框架的关系。

Method: 将ILL中的划分（partition）解释为确定性商变量，规则视为该商变量的边际分布；将一般提升（general lifting）解释为满足所有边际约束的联合分布可行族，特殊提升（special lifting）则通过L2均匀性准则（与最大熵密切相关）实现最大无知重建；采用香农熵提升时，相同约束生成以学习到的抽象为索引的对数线性因子图。

Result: ILL的信息格本身不是贝叶斯网络（其边编码抽象的细化/粗化而非条件依赖），但ILL可被理解为针对可解释约束因子图（基于商变量）的结构学习；在香农熵提升下，ILL等价于最大熵模型。

Conclusion: ILL最佳视角是作为可解释约束因子图的结构学习方法，这厘清了ILL与图模型、最大熵模型的关系，并为推理、可辨识性及混合符号-概率学习开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Information lattice learning (ILL) learns interpretable rules of a signal by alternately projecting the signal onto a partition lattice that encodes a hierarchy of abstractions and lifting selected rules back to the signal domain. When the signal is a probability mass function, we show the probabilistic rules learned by ILL admit a natural probabilistic graphical model (PGM) interpretation and develop this interpretation in detail. A partition in ILL induces a deterministic quotient variable, and a rule is the marginal law of that quotient variable. A rule set is therefore a collection of marginal constraints over interpretable abstractions. General lifting is the feasible family of all joint distributions satisfying those constraints, while special lifting chooses a maximum-ignorance reconstruction, implemented in ILL by an L2 uniformity principle closely related to maximum entropy. Under a Shannon-entropy lifting, the same constraints yield a log-linear factor graph whose factors are indexed by learned abstractions. The information lattice itself, however, is not a Bayesian network: its edges encode refinement and coarsening of abstractions, not conditional dependence. Thus ILL is best viewed as structure learning for interpretable constraint-based factor graphs over quotient variables. This view clarifies how ILL relates to graphical models and maximum entropy models, while suggesting new directions for inference, identifiability, and hybrid symbolic-probabilistic learning.

</details>


### [96] [Physics-Informed Neural Network with Squeeze-Excitation-like Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19853)
*Yun-Fei Song,Long-Gang Pang,Fu-Peng Li,Jun-Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SEA-PINN 是一种融合了 Squeeze-Excitation 注意力机制的物理信息神经网络架构，通过动态重新校准神经元重要性实现高度稳定的初始化，在 20 个基准问题中的 17 个上表现出几乎可忽略的方差和显著降低的初始损失，无需傅里叶特征或周期激活函数即可达到与 TSA-PINN 相当的精度，并可作为轻量级插件模块提升其他 PINN 模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 PINN 存在初始化不稳定、收敛困难以及非线性表达能力不足的问题，尤其在处理高频问题时表现欠佳，亟需一种既能提升稳定性又不增加过多计算负担的方法。

Method: 提出 SEA-PINN 架构，在 PINN 中引入 Squeeze-Excitation 注意力机制，实现跨层神经元重要性的动态重新校准，从而获得准确定性的稳定初始化；无需傅里叶特征嵌入或周期激活函数。

Result: 在 20 个基准问题中 17 个上初始方差几乎可忽略且初始损失显著降低；在高频问题（案例 7）上相对于 FNN-PINN 提升 83%-90%，与专为高频问题设计的 TSA-PINN 精度相当；将 SEA-PINN 集成到 TSA-PINN 中性能提升 42.49%。

Conclusion: SEA-PINN 作为一种轻量级即插即用模块，能够增强非线性表示能力、促进更鲁棒和高效的收敛、提升物理信息学习的整体可靠性。

Abstract: We introduce SEA-PINN, a novel architecture that incorporates a Squeeze-Excitation-like attention mechanism into physics-informed neural networks to dynamically recalibrate the importance of neurons across layers. A key feature of SEA-PINN is its highly stable initialization. On 17 out of 20 benchmark problems, SEA-PINN exhibit nearly negligible variance and significantly reduced initial loss, establishing a quasi-deterministic and favorable starting point for optimization. Notably, without employing Fourier feature embeddings or periodic activation functions, SEA-PINN attained competitive accuracy (83\% vs. 90\% improvement relative to FNN-PINN on the high-frequency case 7) as compared with TSA-PINN-a model specifically engineered for high-frequency problems via learnable frequencies in sinusoidal activations. Furthermore, integrating SEA-PINN into TSA-PINN boosted performance by 42.49\%. These results underscore SEA-PINN as a lightweight plug-in module that enhances nonlinear representation power, promotes more robust and efficient convergence, and strengthens the overall reliability of physics-informed learning.

</details>


### [97] [Weibull Weight-Scale Parameter Evolution under AdamW Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19367)
*Tiexin Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于双参数Weibull框架分析变压器权重分布，推导了AdamW训练中权重尺度参数λ(t)先增长、过冲再松弛的三力分解机制：对齐力（88-94%贡献）、注入力和衰减力。通过自训练Pythia-70M模型验证，并提出样条位移方法从稀疏检查点恢复对齐力（~92-94%精度），同时发现λ(t)峰值与训练数据一致性相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AdamW训练过程中变压器权重分布Weibull尺度参数λ(t)为何呈现先增长、过冲再松弛的动态行为，并揭示其背后的力学机制。

Method: 从AdamW更新推导出权重范数平方的三力分解（对齐力、注入力、衰减力）；在自训练Pythia-70M模型上使用真实优化器矩验证；引入样条位移方法从稀疏检查点恢复对齐力。

Result: 对齐力主导上升阶段（占绝对力预算88-94%）；对齐力与衰减力在饱和期接近平衡；样条位移方法恢复对齐力精度约92-94%，约为两基点基线两倍；λ(t)峰值与训练数据一致性相关。

Conclusion: 成功解释了AdamW训练中权重尺度参数的动态行为，建立了可解释的力学框架，并提供了从实际模型稀疏检查点恢复关键力的实用方法，为理解Transformer训练动力学提供了新视角。

Abstract: Building on a two-parameter Weibull framework for diagnosing transformer weight distributions, we study why the Weibull weight-scale parameter $λ$ grows, overshoots, and then relaxes during AdamW training. We derive a leading-order three-force decomposition of the squared weight norm from the AdamW update: an alignment force measuring the correlation between weights and the adaptive update direction, an injection force from adaptive step magnitude, and a decay force from decoupled weight decay. On self-trained Pythia-70M models with ground-truth optimizer moments, alignment dominates the rise phase, contributing 88-94% of the absolute force budget across four random seeds and remaining robust to super-weight removal. Near saturation, alignment and decay approach balance, explaining the transition from weight-scale growth to relaxation. These force dynamics directly govern the squared-norm component underlying $λ(t)$; the remaining RMS-to-Weibull reconstruction offset is measurable and decomposes into bridge and integration components, totaling approximately 5-6% in densely sampled regions. To extend the analysis to real models where optimizer moments are unavailable, we introduce a spline displacement method that recovers the alignment force from sparse checkpoints with approximately 92-94% accuracy, about twice the naive two-point baseline. We further observe that the peak value of $λ(t)$ varies with training-data coherence in our experiments, suggesting a data-dependent component of weight-scale growth that we leave to a controlled follow-up study. Code and data are available at https://github.com/tiexinding/NPM-Weibull-public.

</details>


### [98] [Zero-Inflated Gaussian Distributions Enable Parameter-Space Sparsity in Estimation-of-Distribution Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19369)
*Andreas Faust,Sven Nitzsche,Juergen Becker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于多元零膨胀高斯分布的分布估计算法（ZIG-EDA），首次将EDA推广到稀疏黑箱优化问题，无需手工设计的稀疏算子，在Lunar Lander基准上表现优于传统EDA和手工稀疏算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EDA无法处理稀疏参数空间（大部分最优系数恰好为零）的黑箱优化问题，而现有的稀疏黑箱优化器不得不重新引入手工设计的稀疏算子、双层优化方案、零阈值等与EDA设计初衷相悖的假设，因此需要一种无需手工算子且能联合优化稀疏模式和活跃值的EDA方法。

Method: 提出多元零膨胀高斯分布（ZIG）作为EDA的采样分布，该分布通过潜变量高斯模型同时表示稀疏模式、活跃参数间的相关性以及二者的交互作用，实现无层次结构的联合优化；引入基于可识别的潜变量参数的反演估计器，从观测样本中恢复潜相关系数结构。

Result: 在Lunar Lander基准上，ZIG-EDA比密集高斯EDA、手工稀疏进化算法和临时稀疏EDA收敛更快、最终回报更高，且找到的控制器仅需少量活跃参数。

Conclusion: ZIG-EDA有效填补了EDA在稀疏黑箱优化领域的空白，证明了通过恰当的概率分布设计可以避免手工稀疏算子，实现稀疏模式与活跃值的联合优化。

Abstract: Estimation-of-distribution algorithms (EDAs) are a powerful class of evolutionary methods for black-box optimization, especially when little is known about the structure of the objective. Whereas classical evolutionary algorithms rely on hand-designed mutation and crossover operators, hard to devise for unknown problem structures, and a source of bias, EDAs sidestep operator design entirely: they fit a probability distribution to the best individuals and sample the next generation from it. EDAs are well established on continuous parameter spaces, but they have not previously been generalized to sparse ones, in which most coefficients of a good solution are exactly zero. Existing sparse black-box optimizers therefore reintroduce exactly what EDAs were designed to avoid: hand-crafted sparsity operators, bi-level schemes alternating between support set and active values, zeroing thresholds, and other baked-in assumptions. We close this gap by proposing multivariate zero-inflated Gaussian (ZIG) distributions as EDA sampling laws. A latent Gaussian model with separate indicator and value dimensions represents sparsity patterns, correlations among active parameters, and the interactions between the two, so sparsity patterns and active values are optimized jointly, hierarchy-free. We show that the latent parameters of this model are identifiable from observed samples, unlike in the missing-data settings where related constructions originate, and introduce practical amortized inversion-based estimators for them. The estimators accurately recover latent correlation structures, and on the Lunar Lander benchmark the resulting ZIG-EDA converges faster and reaches higher final returns than a dense Gaussian EDA, a hand-crafted sparse evolutionary algorithm, and an ad-hoc sparse EDA, while finding controllers with only a small fraction of parameters active.

</details>


### [99] [Human-like autonomy emerges from self-play and a pinch of human data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19370)
*Daphne Cornelisse,Julian Hunt,Zixu Zhang,Waël Doulazmi,Kevin Joseph,Jaime Fernández Fisac,Eugene Vinitsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将少量人类演示数据（仅30分钟）作为正则化目标融入自对弈强化学习的方法，在保持自对弈低成本优势的同时，解决了纯自对弈训练策略与人类驾驶习惯不兼容的问题。该方法仅需2500倍少于模仿学习的人类数据，在单张消费级GPU上15小时即可完成训练，生成的政策能与未见人类轨迹协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯自对弈强化学习训练出的驾驶策略虽然无需人类数据，但容易学习到与人类驾驶习惯不一致的"异类"行为模式；而现有的通过大量奖励工程和域随机化来缓解这种不对齐的方法既脆弱又劳动密集。

Method: 将人类演示数据作为正则化目标，叠加在最小化安全到达目标的奖励之上，实现少量人类数据对自对弈训练过程的约束和引导。

Result: 仅使用30分钟人类演示数据（相当于模仿学习方法数据量的1/2500），生成的策略能与未见过的真实人类轨迹协调，训练时间仅需15小时（单消费级GPU）。

Conclusion: 少量人类数据作为正则化，可以有效解决自对弈强化学习策略与人类行为不对齐的问题，在极低数据成本下实现高效训练和良好的人机协调性能。

Abstract: Self-play reinforcement learning has recently emerged as a way to train driving policies without any human data. It uses cheap, large-scale simulations to substitute expensive, large-scale human driving demonstrations. A key limitation of this approach is that policies trained through pure self-play can learn effective but alien driving conventions incompatible with people. Previous works attempt to mitigate such behavioral misalignments through extensive reward engineering and domain randomization, which are brittle and labor-intensive. Instead of completely discarding human demonstrations, our method treats them as a regularization objective on top of a minimal safe goal-reaching reward. Like the spice in a good stew, we find that a little human data goes a long way: our method uses only 30 minutes of human demonstrations, 2500x fewer than comparable imitation learning approaches. Resulting policies coordinate with held-out human trajectories and complete training in 15 hours on a single consumer-grade GPU. Videos and full source code are available at https://spiced-self-play.com/.

</details>


### [100] [Quantum-classical physics-informed Kolmogorov-Arnold networks for PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20326)
*Xiang Rao,Yuxuan Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了QCPIKAN，一种结合Chebyshev多项式KAN层和参数化量子电路的量子-经典物理信息Kolmogorov-Arnold网络，用于求解偏微分方程。理论证明该设计能加速高频误差以指数速率收敛并有效抑制数值色散。在多孔介质渗流场景（单相流、组分输运、两相流）中，相比现有量子-经典PINN，QCPIKAN在全局预测精度、局部误差控制、动态演化追踪和驱替前沿定位方面均表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子-经典物理信息神经网络在处理复杂PDE时存在高频误差收敛慢、数值色散严重等问题，亟需设计一种兼具高精度与鲁棒性的混合求解框架。

Method: 基于Chebyshev多项式KAN层与参数化量子电路构建混合网络，将物理约束嵌入训练损失以强制执行物理一致性，并利用逼近理论分析误差收敛特性。

Result: 理论证明高频误差以指数速率收敛且数值色散被有效抑制；在多孔介质渗流三种典型场景中，QCPIKAN在全局预测精度、局部误差控制、动态演化追踪和驱替前沿定位方面均优于现有量子-经典PINN。

Conclusion: QCPIKAN为求解复杂PDE提供了一种鲁棒且高效的替代方案，验证了量子-经典混合KAN架构在物理约束学习任务中的潜力。

Abstract: We develop QCPIKAN, the first quantum-classical physics-informed Kolmogorov-Arnold network designed to solve partial differential equations (PDEs). Built upon Chebyshev-polynomial KAN layers and parameterized quantum circuits, this hybrid framework embeds physical constraints into the training loss to enforce physical consistency. Our theoretical investigations grounded in approximation theory prove that this design accelerates high-frequency error convergence to an exponential rate and effectively mitigates numerical dispersion. We validate the framework across three typical seepage scenarios in porous media, including single-phase flow, component transport and two-phase flow. Compared with existing quantum-classical physics-informed neural networks, QCPIKAN achieves superior performance in global prediction accuracy, local error control, dynamic evolution tracking and displacement front localization. This work provides a robust and efficient alternative for solving complex PDEs.

</details>


### [101] [ProMUSE: Progressive Multi-modal Uncertainty-guided Staged Evidential Alzheimer Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19371)
*Long Doan,Branden Chen,Ethan Litton,Huan Huang,Jiajing Huang,Yixin Xie,Weihua Zhou,Nandakumar Narayanan,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProMUSE是一种渐进式多模态不确定性引导的分阶段证据网络，通过自适应判断何时需要额外成像模态（MRI/PET），在保持诊断准确性的同时减少50-90%的高成本影像数据使用，实现资源高效的早期阿尔茨海默病诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病早期诊断高度依赖多模态数据（临床评估、MRI、PET），但MRI和PET成本高昂且非普遍可及，导致全模态推断在真实临床流程中不切实际。需要一种方法在减少昂贵成像依赖的同时保持诊断准确性。

Method: ProMUSE首先使用低成本临床数据进行证据分类，通过狄利克雷主观逻辑模型量化不确定性；当不确定性超过学习阈值时，逐步引入MRI或PET特征，利用Dempster-Shafer理论融合模态层面的信念和不确定性，获得校准的多模态预测。

Result: 在ADNI、AIBL和OASIS数据集上的CN-AD、CN-MCI和MCI-AD任务中，ProMUSE达到与全模态基线相当或更优的准确性，同时将MRI/PET使用量减少50-90%，大幅节省成本。

Conclusion: ProMUSE是一种实用、不确定性感知且资源高效的解决方案，适用于真实世界的阿尔茨海默病筛查，在保持诊断精度的前提下显著降低对昂贵成像模态的依赖。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) is a fatal disorder that destroys memory and cognitive skills in the elderly population. Most treatments for AD are effective in the early stage, leading to an increasing demand for early AD diagnosis. AD diagnosis increasingly relies on multimodal data such as clinical assessments, structural Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI), and Positron Emission Tomography (PET) imaging. However, MRI and PET acquisition remain costly and not universally accessible, making full-modality inference impractical in real-world clinical workflows. We propose ProMUSE, a Progressive Multi-modal Uncertainty Guided Staged Evidential Network that adaptively determines when additional modalities are necessary, helping reduce the overall cost of data acquisition while maintaining accuracy. ProMUSE first performs evidential classification using low-cost clinical data and quantifies uncertainty via a Dirichlet-based subjective logic model. When uncertainty exceeds a learned threshold, ProMUSE progressively incorporates MRI or PET features, fusing modality-wise belief and uncertainty through Dempster-Shafer theory to obtain a calibrated multimodal prediction. This staged acquisition strategy enables accurate diagnosis while minimizing reliance on expensive imaging. Experiments on ADNI, AIBL, and OASIS across CN-AD, CN-MCI, and MCI-AD tasks demonstrate that ProMUSE achieves competitive or superior accuracy compared to full-modality baselines while reducing MRI/PET usage by 50-90%, yielding substantial cost savings. These results highlight ProMUSE as a practical, uncertainty-aware, and resource-efficient solution for real-world AD screening.

</details>


### [102] [Agentic Symbolic Search: Characterizing PDEs Beyond Hand-crafted Expressions, Meshes, and Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20467)
*Zongmin Yu,Liu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASYS是一个先验引导的框架，通过智能体将PDE理论、问题约束和搜索经验转化为可测试的微分符号程序，结合进化搜索和梯度优化自动发现偏微分方程的解析解或近似解析表示，在多个问题中发现了此前未知的封闭形式描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学家通过数学结构理解PDE解，而非数值表格。传统上这依赖人工数学分析，数值模拟和神经网络都无法直接产生这些结构。现有方法缺乏自动化发现PDE解析结构的有效手段。

Method: 提出Agentic Symbolic Search (ASYS)框架：智能体将PDE理论、问题约束和搜索经验翻译为可测试的微分符号程序，数学形式通过进化搜索优化，连续参数通过梯度优化拟合。这是一种自动化的归纳偏置注入而非盲目的符号回归。

Result: 在五个问题（有界动力学、有限时间爆破、自由边界聚焦）上，ASYS产生了可解释的表示，包括Allen-Cahn 2D动力学的几何界面公式和Keller-Segel趋化爆破的九参数收缩律，这些此前没有封闭形式描述。

Conclusion: ASYS展示了一种表征PDE解的新范式，超越了手工解析解、网格数值解和神经网络近似，能够自动化发现PDE的解析结构。

Abstract: Mathematicians understand a PDE solution through mathematical structures rather than tables of computed values. Historically, this has been the product of mathematical analysis, carried out by hand for each problem individually. Neither numerical simulation nor neural networks produce those structures directly. We propose Agentic Symbolic Search (ASYS), a prior-guided framework in which an agent translates PDE theory, public problem constraints, and accumulated search experience into testable differentiable symbolic programs. The mathematical forms are refined under evolutionary search, while their continuous parameters are fit by gradient-based optimization. This makes the search an automated form of inductive-bias injection rather than blind symbolic regression. For problems with known analytical forms, ASYS recovers these forms naturally; for other problems, ASYS constructs analytical approximations which can guide mathematicians toward further analysis. In our experiments, across five problems spanning bounded dynamics, finite-time blow-up, and free-boundary focusing, ASYS produces interpretable representations, including a geometric interface formula for Allen-Cahn 2D dynamics and a nine-parameter contraction law for Keller-Segel chemotactic blow-up, in settings where no closed-form description was previously available. ASYS shows the possibility of a new paradigm for characterizing PDE solutions, beyond handcrafted analytical solutions, mesh-based numerical solutions, and neural network approximations.

</details>


### [103] [cAPM: Continual AI-Assisted Pace-Mapping with Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19373)
*Dylan O'Hara,Pradeep Bajracharya,Casey Meisenzahl,Karli Gillette,Anton J. Prassl,Gernot Plank,Saman Nazarian,Roderick Tung,John L Sapp,Linwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出cAPM，一种持续主动学习框架，用于室性心动过速导管消融中的起搏标测。通过任务无关的代理神经网络、主动学习和持续学习策略，cAPM能跨多个VT靶点迁移知识，以更少的起搏点实现精准定位。在仿真实验中，cAPM仅需4.5个起搏点即可达到81%的临床容忍度定位概率，远超现有方法（13.7个起搏点，38%概率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室性心动过速（VT）的导管消融需要起搏标测来确定干预靶点，但现有方法每遇到一个新的VT靶点都需要重新训练模型，无法跨靶点或跨患者迁移先前积累的起搏标测知识，导致标测效率低下、起搏次数过多。因此，作者旨在构建一种能持续积累并迁移知识的AI辅助起搏标测方法。

Method: cAPM包含三个核心组件：(1) 任务无关的代理神经网络，学习从起搏位点到12导联ECG形态的映射；(2) 主动学习策略，为每个新VT靶点选择信息量最大的起搏位点以优化代理模型；(3) 持续学习策略，在顺序处理多个VT靶点时保持对先前靶点知识的记忆，防止灾难性遗忘。可额外使用记忆回放（replay）来增强知识保留。

Result: 在包含不同生理条件和心室几何形状的仿真测试平台上，cAPM（无论是否使用回放）平均仅需4.5个起搏标测点，即可达到81%的概率在临床容忍度（5 mm精度）内定位VT靶点。相比之下，现有最优主动学习方法需要13.7个起搏点，定位概率仅为38%。

Conclusion: cAPM通过持续学习和主动学习相结合，显著减少了起搏标测所需的采样点数并提高了定位精度，为后续向活体动物实验和临床研究的转化奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Ventricular tachycardia is a life-threatening rhythm disorder and a major cause of sudden cardiac death. Pace-mapping is a clinical procedure for identifying the intervention target during catheter ablation of VT. It requires clinicians to pace different sites in the ventricles and rapidly interpret the resulting electrocardiograms to determine where to pace next or whether a target site has been identified. Active learning AI models have been proposed to guide clinicians to the next pacing site, showing promise in reducing the number of pacing sites and improving the efficiency of pace-mapping. Existing methods require retraining each target without the ability to transfer knowledge across multiple VTs within the same patient or across patients. We introduce cAPM for continuous AI-assisted pace-mapping to capture and transfer knowledge accumulated from past pace-mapping data to reduce the number of pace-mapping data needed for future target VTs. This is made possible by a task-agnostic surrogate neural network that learns the mapping from pacing sites to 12-lead ECG morphology, an active-learning strategy that refines this surrogate model by selecting the most informative pacing site for each target, and a continual learning strategy to do so sequentially while retaining knowledge from prior targets. Evaluated on an in-silico testbed consisting of sequentially-presented localization tasks across different physiological conditions and ventricular geometries, cAPM with and without replay of past data samples achieved an 81% probability of localizing within clinical tolerance (5 mm accuracy) using 4.5 pace-mapping sites, compared to the state-of-the-art active-learning method achieving 38% probability using 13.7 pacing sites. These results provide a strong basis for preparing cAPM towards in-vivo preclinical and clinical studies where it can be used to guide pace-mapping.

</details>


### [104] [Protein Representation Learning with Secondary-Structure and Energy-Filtered Hydrogen-Bond Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19374)
*Mohamed Mouhajir,Limei Wang,El Houcine Bergou,Hajar El Hammouti,Lamiae Azizi,Dongqi Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出二级结构感知的图神经网络SSProNet，通过引入二级结构赋值和能量过滤的氢键拓扑构建图边，在蛋白质表示学习中取得一致改进，并增强生物可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的蛋白质表示方法主要依赖序列邻接或几何接近性，未能充分反映蛋白质折叠原理；蛋白质的实际三维构象围绕二级结构元素（α-螺旋和β-折叠）组织，这些结构编码了重复出现的局部基序和稳定的氢键相互作用。

Method: 构建二级结构感知的图神经网络：残基级节点表示增强二级结构赋值；图边由能量滤波后的氢键相互作用构建；通过该设计同时捕获局部结构上下文和长程耦合。

Result: 在常用蛋白质基准上相比现有图方法取得一致改进；学习到的图连接性与已建立的结构基序对齐，增强了生物可解释性。

Conclusion: 引入二级结构和能量滤波的氢键拓扑为蛋白质表示学习提供了有效的归纳偏置。

Abstract: Graph-based representations are widely used in protein modeling, yet many existing approaches rely primarily on sequence adjacency or geometric proximity, which only partially reflect the principles governing protein folding. Proteins instead adopt complex three-dimensional conformations organized around secondary structure elements, such as $α$-helices and $β$-sheets, which encode recurring local motifs and stabilizing hydrogen-bond interactions. In this work, we introduce a secondary-structure-aware graph neural network for protein representation learning. Residue-level node representations are augmented with secondary structure assignments, and graph edges are constructed from hydrogen-bond interactions filtered by their energetic strength. This design enables the model to capture both local structural context and long-range couplings that are central to protein stability and function. We evaluate the proposed approach on commonly used protein benchmarks and observe consistent improvements over existing graph-based methods. In addition, the resulting graph representations offer enhanced biological interpretability, as the learned connectivity aligns with established structural motifs. These findings suggest that incorporating secondary structure and energy-filtered hydrogen-bond topology provides an effective inductive bias for protein representation learning. The code is released at https://github.com/mohamedmohamed2021/SSProNet

</details>


### [105] [Physics-Informed Discovery of Yield Functions in Plasticity via Convex Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19375)
*Hyeonbin Moon,Donghyuk Cho,Jecheon Yu,Jeong Whan Yoon,Seunghwa Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种物理信息框架，无需应力观测、塑性应变测量或预设参数化形式，即可从全场位移和反力数据中通过可微应力更新和物理信息力平衡损失识别各向异性屈服函数，采用凸神经网络表示屈服函数，并通过FE基准测试验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向异性屈服函数的识别面临三大挑战：屈服现象无法直接观测、方向性标定需要大量加载方向、合适的解析形式选择困难。现有方法依赖应力观测或直接屈服面数据，缺乏从全场位移和力数据直接识别屈服函数的途径。

Method: 框架将屈服函数作为弹塑性应力积分中的力学约束本构组件进行识别，而非通过应力空间直接监督。屈服函数由凸神经网络表示，强制执行凸性和一阶正齐次性，并施加拉压对称性。通过可微应力更新和多个加载工况下的物理信息力平衡损失进行训练。

Result: 采用von Mises、Hill 1948和Yld2000-2d屈服函数的FE基准研究验证了框架，评估了屈服轮廓一致性、位移噪声敏感性、塑性活跃应力状态的可识别性、认知不确定性和多项式代理部署。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一条力学约束路径，可从位移和力数据中发现各向异性屈服函数，并将识别出的组件保持在弹塑性应力积分的结构内。

Abstract: Identifying anisotropic yield functions remains challenging since yielding is not directly observed in full-field mechanical measurements, directional calibration can require many loading directions, and selecting an appropriate analytical form is nontrivial. This study proposes a physics-informed framework for discovering yield functions from full-field displacement data and reaction force data, without stress observations, plastic strain measurements, direct yield surface data, or a prescribed parametric yield function. The framework identifies the yield function as a mechanically constrained constitutive component inside elastoplastic stress integration, rather than through direct stress-space supervision. The yield function is represented by a convex neural network that enforces convexity and positive homogeneity of degree one while imposing the assumed tension-compression symmetry, and this neural yield function is trained with a differentiable stress update and a physics-informed force equilibrium loss across multiple loading cases. The proposed framework is validated using finite element (FE) benchmark studies with von Mises, Hill 1948, and Yld2000-2d yield functions, assessing yield contour agreement, displacement-noise sensitivity, identifiability through plastically active stress states, epistemic uncertainty, and polynomial-surrogate deployment. This study provides a mechanics-constrained pathway for discovering anisotropic yield functions from displacement and force data while keeping the identified component within the structure of elastoplastic stress integration.

</details>


### [106] [Cost-Optimal LLM Routing with Limited User Feedback under User Satisfaction Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19376)
*Herbert Woisetschläger,Arastun Mammadli,Ryan Zhang,Shiqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SLARouter是一种在线路由算法，能在仅依赖生产系统中稀疏、单侧用户反馈的情况下，学习成本最优策略，同时提供严格的服务水平协议（SLA）合规保障，相比现有方法可降低高达2.2倍运营成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理成本快速增长，用户对高质量响应的需求（通过SLA正式规定）与成本之间存在根本性矛盾，而现有成本感知路由方法依赖完整反馈信号、离线训练、大量工作负载调优，且缺乏SLA保障和推理时自适应性。

Method: 提出SLARouter，一种在线路由算法，利用生产系统中稀疏、单侧的用户反馈来学习成本最优策略，并提供成本最优性和严格SLA合规的理论保证。

Result: 在多种LLM基准测试中，SLARouter在不需逐基准调优的情况下满足SLA约束，相比现有基线方法降低运营成本达2.2倍。

Conclusion: SLARouter有效解决了LLM推理中成本与SLA质量保障之间的权衡问题，具备理论保证和实际部署优势。

Abstract: Inference costs for large language model (LLM) applications are rapidly growing, driven by surging demand and rising infrastructure cost. Users expect high-quality responses, and in commercial settings this is formally codified in Service Level Agreements (SLAs), creating a fundamental tension between cost and quality. Recent progress on cost-aware LLM request routing has shown potential to resolve this tension, but existing approaches rely on complete feedback signals, offline training, extensive per-workload tuning, and most lack SLA guarantees or inference-time adaptivity. We introduce SLARouter, an online routing algorithm that learns a cost-optimal policy from the sparse, one-sided user feedback available in production systems. SLARouter provides theoretical guarantees for both cost optimality and strict SLA compliance. Experiments across a wide range of LLM benchmarks show that SLARouter satisfies SLA constraints without the need for per-benchmark tuning, reducing operating cost by up to 2.2x over existing baselines.

</details>


### [107] [Emyx: Fast and efficient all-atom protein generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19377)
*Nicholas J. Williams,Ward Haddadin,Matteo P. Ferla,Constantin Schneider,Nicholas B. Woodall,Ruby Sedgwick,Christian D. Madsen,Andrew L. Hopkins,Edward O. Pyzer-Knapp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Emyx是一个仅140M参数的流匹配模型，通过轻量条件表示和稀疏连接替代复杂架构，在训练成本降低4倍的情况下，在酶设计基准上全面超越Proteína-Complexa和RFdiffusion3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的全原子生成模型继承了结构预测中昂贵的架构，导致训练成本高、样本多样性有限。作者认为生成模型只需条件于稀疏几何约束而非丰富的共进化信号，因此可以大幅简化架构。

Method: Emyx采用140M参数的条件流匹配模型，用标准Transformer模块替代繁重的嵌入堆栈，使用轻量条件表示和稀疏连接。此外，推导了流匹配插值项到EDM噪声水平框架的精确重参数化，将流匹配训练效率与扩散模型的最先进采样方法桥接。

Result: Emyx在AME酶设计基准上，在全局折叠恢复、催化几何精度、结构新颖性、骨架多样性和几何有效性等严格评估指标上全面超越Proteína-Complexa和RFdiffusion3。训练仅需682 GPU小时，约为RFdiffusion3的1/4。

Conclusion: 生成模型可以大幅简化架构设计，在降低计算成本的同时获得更好性能，为计算酶设计提供了更高效的新范式。

Abstract: Computational enzyme design requires generating proteins that scaffold catalytic residues and ligands, a task that demands both geometric accuracy and structural diversity from the underlying generative model. Current all-atom generators inherit expensive architectures from structure prediction, leading to high training costs and limited sample diversity. We argue that much of this complexity is unnecessary for generators, which condition on sparse geometric constraints rather than rich co-evolutionary signals. Emyx is a 140M-parameter conditional flow matching model that concentrates capacity within standard transformer blocks, replacing heavy embedding stacks with lightweight conditional representations and sparse connectivity. We additionally derive an exact reparametrisation of the flow matching interpolant into the EDM noise-level framework, bridging flow matching training efficiency with state-of-the-art sampling methods designed for diffusion models without retraining. Despite being the smallest model, Emyx outperforms both Proteína-Complexa and RFdiffusion3 against the AME enzyme design benchmark across success rate under strict evaluation requiring both global fold recovery and catalytic geometry accuracy, structural novelty, scaffold diversity, and geometric validity, while training in just $682$ GPU-hours, roughly $4\times$ less than RFdiffusion3.

</details>


### [108] [A Hybrid GNN-FEM Framework for Phase-Field Fracture Simulation. Physics-Preserving Hybridization for Generalizable Surrogate Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19378)
*Hyeonbin Moon,Yongjin Choi,Seunghwa Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合GNN-FEM框架，将图神经网络代理集成到传统交错格式中，替代每个载荷增量下的相场更新，同时保留FEM位移求解器以强制执行力学平衡和边界条件，实现了高效且可泛化的相场断裂模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场方法虽然为复杂裂纹演化提供了稳健的变分框架，但求解耦合、非线性且历史依赖的系统需在增量有限元过程中进行，计算成本极高，限制了实际应用。

Method: 将图神经网络代理集成到交错求解方案中，替代相场更新步骤，保留FEM位移求解器；采用无量纲特征设计、基于网格的图表示和物理信息损失函数（来自控制相场方程），仅学习增量步骤而非完整轨迹。

Result: 相比传统FEM，混合方法在降低计算成本的同时保持精度，并在不同几何形状、加载条件、材料属性和离散化下展现出鲁棒的预测性能。

Conclusion: 选择性代理策略强调识别具有物理意义和增量结构的学习目标，而非通过暴力数据生成学习完整断裂过程，是实现高效且可泛化SciML的关键。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning (SciML) has emerged as a promising approach for accelerating simulations of complex physical systems, yet achieving physically consistent and generalizable predictions for nonlinear, history-dependent problems remains a central challenge. In this study, we propose a hybrid GNN--FEM framework for efficient and generalizable phase-field fracture modeling. While phase-field approaches provide a robust variational framework for simulating complex crack evolution, their high computational cost limits practical applications because they require solving coupled, nonlinear, and history-dependent systems within an incremental finite element procedure. To address this challenge, a graph neural network surrogate is integrated into the conventional staggered scheme, replacing the phase-field update at each load increment while retaining the FEM-based displacement solver to enforce mechanical equilibrium and boundary conditions. By preserving the incremental solution structure, the framework remains consistent with history-dependent fracture evolution without requiring the surrogate to approximate the full solution trajectory. This selective surrogate strategy emphasizes the identification of a physically meaningful and incrementally structured learning target, rather than relying on brute-force data generation to learn the full fracture process. The proposed framework achieves strong generalization across varying geometries, loading conditions, material properties, and discretizations through dimensionless feature design, a graph-based formulation on mesh-based domains, and a physics-informed loss derived from the governing phase-field equation. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the hybrid approach reduces computational cost while maintaining accuracy compared with conventional FEM, and exhibits robust predictive performance across diverse problem settings.

</details>


### [109] [How Linear Is a Transformer Feed-Forward Block? Per-Block Linear Recoverability Is Learned, Not Architectural](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19379)
*Stuart Whipp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出线性可恢复性（R²_lin）指标，通过最小二乘线性近似分解FFN块，发现不同模型和块间的非线性程度高度异质且非单调，该指标可用于指导模型压缩并揭示训练线性基线的收敛陷阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer中的FFN块通常被视为非线性计算单元，但其实际训练后的非线性程度从未被系统量化测量，缺乏一个无优化器的线性度评估指标。

Method: 将每个FFN视为逐位置的输入-输出映射，分解为精确的最小二乘线性近似加残差，用线性映射解释的留出方差定义线性可恢复性（R²_lin），并通过低秩双线性探针分析残差结构。

Result: GPT-2、Pythia-160m、llama-160m的12个块中R²_lin高度异质且非单调（0.3到0.99），相同宽度的GELU模型轮廓差异显著；低秩双线性探针仅恢复少量R²且增益与残差非线性无关；可恢复块可被8倍参数缩减的单层替换（GPT-2早期FFN仅+0.77困惑度），低可恢复块标记不安全压缩位置；训练线性基线在病态Transformer激活上严重欠收敛。

Conclusion: R²_lin是学习到的块级属性而非架构固有属性，可作为无优化器的线性度度量、有指导的压缩信号，并揭示了使用训练线性基线的方法论陷阱，建议使用闭式最小二乘上界。

Abstract: Transformer feed-forward networks (FFNs) are often treated as nonlinear stores of computation, yet how nonlinear a trained FFN block actually is has rarely been measured. We treat each FFN as a position-wise input-to-output map and split it into the exact least-squares linear approximation plus a residual. The held-out variance the closed-form linear map explains defines a block's linear recoverability (R^2_lin), an optimiser-free measure of its linearity.
  Across all twelve blocks of GPT-2, Pythia-160m, and llama-160m, R^2_lin is highly heterogeneous and non-monotone with depth, ranging from near-linear (>0.99) to strongly nonlinear (<0.3) between adjacent blocks, and is not set by the activation function: same-width GELU models GPT-2 and Pythia-160m have sharply different profiles, so recoverability is a learned property of individual trained blocks, not an architectural one. A low-rank bilinear probe of the residual recovers only a few points of R^2, with gain uncorrelated with residual nonlinearity: the unrecovered computation is not a single position-wise product but higher-order or distributed structure.
  The measurement also serves as a targeted compression signal: recoverable blocks admit large single-layer replacements (GPT-2's early FFN at 8x fewer parameters for +0.77 perplexity), while low-recoverability blocks flag where this is unsafe. It further exposes a methodological pitfall: trained linear baselines can badly under-converge on ill-conditioned transformer activations, so we report the exact closed-form least-squares ceiling throughout.

</details>


### [110] [VERITAS: Verifier-Guided Proof Search for Zero-Shot Formal Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19399)
*Manish Acharya,Zhenyu Liao,Yueke Zhang,Kevin Leach,Yu Huang,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VERITAS是一个零样本框架，通过两阶段协议（Best-of-N采样+批评引导的MCTS）将验证器信号反馈到证明搜索中，在miniF2F上达到40.6%，并发布了包含55个定理的组合学基准VERITAS-CombiBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的形式化证明器通常将丰富的验证器信号（语法错误、类型不匹配、部分目标进展）简化为二元的通过/失败信号，导致大量有用信息被浪费。

Method: 提出VERITAS两阶段协议：Phase 1进行Best-of-N采样；Phase 2使用批评引导的MCTS，将Phase 1的失败作为显式负例输入，引导探索。

Result: miniF2F上40.6%（Best-of-5为36.9%，Portfolio为26.2%）；VERITAS-CombiBench上7.3%（Best-of-5为1.8%，Portfolio为3.6%）。

Conclusion: 验证器反馈的细粒度利用能显著提升形式化证明性能，尤其在需要从反馈中迭代恢复正确引理名的场景下，无引导采样反而有害。

Abstract: LLM-based formal provers often collapse rich verifier signals (syntax errors, type mismatches, partial goal progress) into a binary pass/fail bit. We present VERITAS, a zero-shot framework that routes every verifier signal back into proof search through a two-phase protocol: Best-of-N sampling first, then a critic-guided MCTS pass that ingests Phase 1 failures as explicit negative examples. The protocol preserves every theorem solved by its own Phase 1 sweep, so Phase 2's additional solves are attributable to feedback-driven exploration. VERITAS reaches 40.6% on miniF2F (vs. an independently run Best-of-5 at 36.9%, Portfolio 26.2%) and 7.3% on VERITAS-CombiBench, a 55-theorem combinatorics benchmark we release on which Best-of-5 (1.8%) falls below Portfolio (3.6%), exposing that unguided sampling hurts when correct lemma names must be recovered iteratively from verifier feedback. Artifacts are available on GitHub.

</details>


### [111] [Thermodynamic Signatures of Reasoning: Free-Energy and Spectral-Form-Factor Diagnostics for Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19404)
*Salim Khazem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Free-Energy Signatures (Fes)，一种基于注意力图拉普拉斯算子的谱描述符，通过热力学势和随机矩阵理论特征检测大语言模型的幻觉，在六个模型和六个基准上平均比LapEig提升+6.5 AUROC点，比GoR-4提升+2.4 AUROC点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于注意力谱的幻觉检测方法仅使用少量特征值或手工标量来概括拉普拉斯谱，忽略了谱的大部分结构信息，导致检测精度有限。

Method: 将每层注意力拉普拉斯视为哈密顿量，提取其热力学势（配分函数、自由能、谱熵、热容）和随机矩阵理论谱形因子，构建Fes描述符；理论证明了Lipschitz稳定性、表达丰富性以及PAC可学习性边界。

Result: 在六个开源LLM和六个基准上，基于Fes的轻量级探测器平均AUROC达到最优，比LapEig提升+6.5点，比GoR-4提升+2.4点；无监督RMT偏差得分平均AUROC为0.71；RMT分析表明正确生成呈现Wigner-Dyson谱统计，幻觉呈现Poisson谱统计。

Conclusion: Fes是一种有效且无需修改底层LLM的幻觉检测方法，利用更完整的谱信息显著提升了检测性能，同时为理解幻觉的谱特性提供了理论视角。

Abstract: Hallucination detection in large language models (LLMs) is deployment-critical, and recent work shows that the spectrum of attention-derived graph Laplacians carries strong signal about reasoning quality. Prior spectral diagnostics, however, summarize the Laplacian spectrum by a handful of eigenvalues or hand-picked scalars, leaving most of its structure unused. We propose Free-Energy Signatures (Fes), a spectral descriptor that treats each layer's attention Laplacian as a Hamiltonian and extracts its thermodynamic potentials partition function, free energy, spectral entropy, heat capacity together with the random-matrix-theory (RMT) spectral form factor. We prove three results: (i)~Lipschitz stability of Fes under attention perturbation; (ii)~an expressiveness result showing that Fes enriches finite spectral summaries and approximates moment-derived spectral functionals under explicit regularity and grid-resolution assumptions; and (iii)~a finite-sample PAC bound on the AUROC of a training-free detector built from Fes. Empirically, across six open-weight LLMs and six benchmarks, a lightweight probe on Fes descriptors achieves the strongest aggregate AUROC among attention-spectral baselines, improving over LapEig by $+6.5$ AUROC points and over GoR-4 by $+2.4$ points on average, while requiring no update to the underlying LLM. In the fully unsupervised setting, an RMT-deviation score achieves mean AUROC $0.71$, providing a label-free but weaker detector. A complementary RMT analysis shows that correct generations exhibit more Wigner-Dyson like spectral statistics, whereas hallucinations exhibit more Poisson-like statistics. The anonymized code and config are provided in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [112] [FlexLAM: Resolving the Bottleneck Trade-off in Latent Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19408)
*Takanori Yoshimoto,Yang Hu,Naruya Kondo,Tatsuya Matsushima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlexLAM提出用可变长度潜在动作替代固定容量瓶颈，通过嵌套dropout训练生成前缀有效编码，在不改变架构的情况下匹配或超越固定容量LAM的性能，并支持推理时动态调整token预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在动作模型（LAMs）存在固定容量瓶颈的权衡：过紧的编码会丢弃动作对齐所需的过渡线索，过松的编码则在标注稀缺时引入额外变化难以解析。需要一种无需新架构或损失函数即可自适应调整容量的方案。

Method: FlexLAM采用可变长度潜在动作，通过嵌套dropout（nested dropout）训练生成前缀有效（prefix-valid）编码，优先捕获紧凑的过渡结构，仅在需要时添加细节，无需新增架构或损失函数。

Result: 单个FlexLAM在标准稀疏标注监督和低回报单任务对齐压力测试中，在每个评估的token预算下均匹配或超越分别训练的固定容量LAM；支持推理时动态调整token预算而无需重新训练；在Ego4D过渡重建任务上取得改进。

Conclusion: 可变长度潜在动作是无架构依赖、即插即用的固定容量瓶颈替代方案，可应用于潜在动作模型、潜在动作世界模型和视频预训练动作接口。

Abstract: Latent actions provide a compact interface between action-free video and downstream decision-making, yet existing Latent Action Models (LAMs) force every transition through a fixed-capacity bottleneck. We identify a bottleneck trade-off: overly tight codes can discard transition cues needed for action alignment, while overly loose codes preserve additional transition variation that must be resolved when alignment labels are scarce or narrowly distributed. FlexLAM replaces this fixed capacity with variable-length latent actions trained by nested dropout, yielding prefix-valid codes that capture compact transition structure first and add detail only when needed, without new architectures or losses. A single FlexLAM matches or surpasses separately trained fixed-capacity LAMs at every evaluated token budget under standard scarce-label supervision and under a low-return single-task alignment stress test, indicating that FlexLAM is not merely adjustable at inference time but learns a better latent-action interface at the same token budgets. The same model supports inference-time token-budget adjustment without retraining, and FlexLAM improves Ego4D transition reconstruction. These results suggest that variable-length latent actions are an architecture-free, drop-in upgrade to the fixed-capacity bottleneck in latent action models, latent-action world models, and video-pretrained action interfaces.

</details>


### [113] [Spectral DPPs via NEPv: A Scalable Continuous Relaxation of Determinantal MAP for Diversity-Aware Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19411)
*Richard Yi Da Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将NP难的DPP-MAP子集选择问题重新表述为Stiefel流形上的连续优化问题，导出一种新型非线性特征值问题（NEPv），并设计自洽场（SCF）迭代求解算法（OurMethod），该算法仅需矩阵-向量乘积，在近线性时间O((ndk+nk²)t)内运行，适用于大规模数据筛选场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习中（数据筛选、核心集选择、主动学习批次获取、上下文学习示例选择等）需要从海量候选中选取小而多样且高质量的子集，DPP的MAP目标虽能提供原则性的多样性度量，但其精确求解是NP难的，且现有贪心/采样算法在n达百万至十亿规模时计算成本过高，无法满足数据中心化场景的需求。

Method: 将DPP-MAP重新表述为Stiefel流形上的连续优化问题，证明其一阶最优性条件构成一种具有特征向量依赖性的新型非线性特征值问题（NEPv）；针对该NEPv设计自洽场（SCF）迭代，并给出基于谱隙的局部收缩保证；算法仅需矩阵-向量乘积，复杂度为O((ndk+nk²)t)，t为迭代次数。

Result: 所提算法OurMethod在理论上具有近线性时间缩放性，可集成低秩与特征映射核，显著降低了现有DPP-MAP求解方法在大规模数据上的计算开销；但本文侧重理论分析，未包含完整真实数据基准测试。

Conclusion: 通过连续松弛和NEPv视角，为大规模DPP-MAP子集选择提供了可扩展的迭代求解框架，在数据中心的机器学习应用中具有潜力；实证验证留待后续工作。

Abstract: Selecting a small, diverse, high-quality subset from a massive pool of candidates is a recurring primitive in modern machine learning -- data curation and coreset selection for training and fine-tuning large models, active-learning batch acquisition, prompt and exemplar selection for in-context learning, retrieval diversification, and experimental design. Determinantal Point Processes (\DPP s) give a principled, well-calibrated notion of diversity for this task, but their \emph{MAP} objective -- pick a size-$k$ subset $S$ maximizing $\logdet(L_S)$ -- is NP-hard, and the standard greedy and sampling algorithms scale superlinearly in the ground-set size $n$. This cost is prohibitive precisely in the data-centric regime where diversity matters most, where $n$ ranges over millions to billions of candidate examples, features, or embeddings. We recast \DPP-MAP as a continuous optimization problem over the Stiefel manifold, and show that its first-order optimality conditions form a \emph{Nonlinear Eigenvalue Problem with eigenvector dependency} (\NEPv) of a previously unstudied form. This \NEPv\ admits a self-consistent field (\SCF) iteration with a spectral-gap-based local contraction guarantee, giving a principled iterative solver where the diversity objective drives an eigenvector-dependent operator. The resulting algorithm, \OurMethod, requires only matrix-vector products with the kernel and runs in time $O\!\big((ndk+nk^2)\,t\big)$ for a small number of iterations $t$, scaling near-linearly in $n$ and integrating directly with low-rank and feature-map kernels common in ML. This paper focuses on the relaxation, solver, and scaling analysis; full real-data benchmarking is left to a planned empirical study.

</details>


### [114] [Spectral Retrieval-Augmented Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19412)
*Huu Hiep Nguyen,Minh Hoang Nguyen,Dung Nguyen,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SpecReTF，一种将时间序列转换为窗口频率表示、使用结合幅度和相位的相似度度量、并应用指数移动平均加权来平衡近期与历史上下文的检索增强时间序列预测方法，在多个基准数据集上超越了传统时域检索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强的时间序列预测方法存在两个根本性局限：频谱盲区（忽略频域周期性结构特征）和时间近因偏差（对历史数据等权处理，未强调近期更相关的模式）。

Method: 将时间序列转换为窗口频率表示，使用结合幅度和相位信息的组合度量衡量相似度，并应用指数移动平均加权方案以平衡近期与历史上下文。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，SpecReTF优于时域检索方法，在多样化的非平稳时间序列上取得了更优的预测精度。

Conclusion: SpecReTF通过引入频域信息和时间加权机制，有效克服了现有检索方法的频谱盲区和时间近因偏差问题，显著提升了非平稳时间序列的预测性能。

Abstract: Time series forecasting leverages historical patterns to predict future values, but traditional methods face challenges when dealing with complex, non-stationary patterns that are difficult to memorize during training. Retrieval-augmented approaches have emerged as promising solutions by retrieving similar historical patterns to enhance predictions. However, existing retrieval methods suffer from two fundamental limitations: spectral blindness, which overlooks critical frequency-domain characteristics that capture underlying periodic structures, and temporal recency, which treats all historical data equally without emphasizing recent, more relevant patterns. In this paper, we propose SpecReTF, a novel retrieval method that addresses these issues by converting time series into windowed frequency representations, measuring similarity with a combined metric that captures both amplitude and phase information. To balance recency and historical context, we apply an exponential moving average weighting scheme that emphasizes recent windows. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that SpecReTF outperforms time-domain retrieval methods, achieving superior forecasting accuracy across diverse, non-stationary time series.

</details>


### [115] [Does Text Actually Help? Uncovering and Resolving Text Collapse in Multimodal Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19413)
*Huu Hiep Nguyen,Minh Hoang Nguyen,Dung Nguyen,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现多模态时间序列预测中文本分支存在“文本坍缩”问题（文本分支退化为内容无关的变换），并提出REST-TS方法，通过将数值主干独立预测，文本分支专门监督预测残差（数值无法解释的差距），迫使文本提取真实内容，实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态时间序列预测框架中，文本分支存在严重的“文本坍缩”问题——文本分支退化为内容无关的变换，无论输入何种描述都贡献可忽略的判别信号。根本原因在于时间序列预测中数值输入与输出存在强自相关，导致数值主干天然占主导，而文本分支即便携带互补关键信息也被系统性利用不足。

Method: 提出REST-TS（Residual-Exclusive Supervision for Text in Time Series）：将数值与文本之间的不对称性转化为设计原则——数值主干独立生成数值预测，文本分支被专门监督用于预测残差（数值无法解释的预测差距）。由于数值路径无法减少这些损失，文本分支被迫从输入描述中提取真实内容。

Result: 在多种真实世界领域和骨干架构上，REST-TS取得了SOTA性能，并且在文本分支利用率上显著优于现有框架，实证表明对文本分支在残差上进行监督能迫使其提取输入中的真实内容。

Conclusion: REST-TS通过将数值-文本不对称性（数值自相关主导，文本利用不足）转化为设计优势，有效解决了文本坍缩问题，使文本分支发挥应有的互补作用，在多模态时间序列预测中取得更好的性能。

Abstract: Multimodal time series forecasting, which pairs numerical sequences with domain-relevant textual reports, promises to inject world knowledge into forecasting pipelines. However, we uncover a critical failure mode in existing frameworks that we term text collapse: the text branch converges to a content-independent transformation, contributing negligible discriminative signal regardless of the input description. We argue that text collapse is a consequence of a fundamental asymmetry in time series forecasting: the numerical input is strongly autocorrelated with the output, making the numerical backbone inherently dominant, while the text branch, despite carrying complementary and often critical information, is insufficiently utilized, leading to its systematic underexploitation. To address this, we propose \textbf{REST-TS} (\textbf{R}esidual-\textbf{E}xclusive \textbf{S}upervision for \textbf{T}ext in \textbf{T}ime \textbf{S}eries), which turns the asymmetry into a design principle: the numerical backbone produces its own independent numerical forecast, and the text branch is exclusively supervised to predict the structured components of the residual, the prediction gap that numbers cannot explain. Because no numerical pathway can reduce these losses, the text branch must extract genuine content from the input description. Evaluated across diverse real-world domains and backbone architectures, REST-TS achieves state-of-the-art performance and consistently demonstrates greater text-branch utilization than existing frameworks, providing strong empirical evidence that supervising the text branch on the residual compels it to extract genuine content from the input.

</details>


### [116] [MortarBench: Evaluating Mortgage Loan Origination Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19416)
*Matthew Toles,Yunan Lu,Manav Munjal,Bojun Liu,Yuanhao Deng,Stephanie Selig,Derek Rindner,Cheng Li,Zhou Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MortarBench是首个贷款发起智能体基准测试，发现现有LLM性能不佳（最高77.1%准确率）且存在对外国名字的系统性偏见；提出的CRIT置信度校准框架可将准确率提升至80.5%，同时改善风险管理并减少偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有公司开始使用AI智能体辅助贷款发起流程，但缺乏公开基准来评估其性能，因此需要构建一个标准化的评估框架。

Method: 1) 构建MortarBench基准：采用金融数据合成与变异管道生成覆盖边缘案例且符合真实分布的数据；2) 提出CRIT置信度校准框架来提升模型性能。

Result: 闭源LLM在MortarBench上最高准确率为77.1%；CRIT框架将准确率提升至80.5%，同时改善风险管理导向并减少偏见。

Conclusion: 现有LLM在贷款发起任务中表现不足且存在偏见，CRIT框架能有效提升性能、改善风险控制并缓解偏见问题。

Abstract: Loan origination is the process by which a lender creates a new loan, from application and underwriting through approval and funding. This process serves a critical role in evaluating the eligibility and level of risk posed by an applicant. Recently, firms have begun using mortgage loan agents to augment human loan officers, despite a lack of any public benchmark. To fill this gap, we present MortarBench, a loan origination agent benchmark. MortarBench uses a financial data synthesis and mutation pipeline to generate examples with broad edge case coverage that match real-world distributions and questions. We find that state-of-the-art large language models (LLMs) perform poorly, with closed-source models achieving at most 77.1\% exact match accuracy. We also discover systematic biases in LLM perception of foreignness related to non-English names. Noting these weaknesses, we introduce CRIT, a confidence calibration framework. Our method increases accuracy to 80.5\% while improving risk management steering and reducing bias.

</details>


### [117] [3D-DLP: Self-Supervised 3D Object-Centric Scene Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19451)
*Ellina Zhang,Madhaven Iyengar,Amir Zadeh,Chuan Li,Deepak Pathak,David Held,Tal Daniel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出3D-DLP，一种自监督的3D物体中心表示学习模型，将场景级RGB-D或体素观测分解为一组3D潜粒子的集合，每个粒子编码了3D关键点位置、边界框尺寸和外观特征等解耦属性，通过端到端自监督重建目标学习可解释的逐粒子分割图，并在模拟和真实数据集上展示了潜空间的可解释性和可控性，以及在下游机器人操作任务中的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物体中心表示学习大多基于2D图像，缺乏对3D空间结构的显式建模；而直接使用密集3D输入（如点云）虽保留空间信息但缺乏物体中心结构且内存开销大。因此需要一种既能显式建模3D结构又能保持物体中心紧凑表示的模型。

Method: 基于Deep Latent Particles (DLP)框架扩展至3D，将RGB-D或体素观测编码为一组3D潜粒子，每个粒子包含解耦属性：3D关键点位置、边界框尺寸和外观特征；通过自监督重建目标学习逐粒子分割图。

Result: 在模拟和真实数据集上验证了潜空间的可解释性和可控性（通过操纵粒子位置可生成新场景）；在下游机器人操作任务中，基于3D潜粒子的方法优于缺乏显式3D信息或使用内存密集型密集3D输入的基线方法。

Conclusion: 3D-DLP成功将物体中心表示学习从2D扩展到3D，实现了可解释、可控的3D场景表示，并在机器人操作等下游任务中展现出实用价值，代码已开源。

Abstract: We introduce 3D-DLP, a self-supervised object-centric representation learning model that decomposes scene-level RGB-D or voxel observations into a set of 3D latent particles. Building on the Deep Latent Particles (DLP) framework, each particle encodes disentangled attributes, including 3D keypoint position, bounding box dimensions, and appearance features, and represents a distinct entity in the scene. The model learns interpretable per-particle segmentation maps through an end-to-end self-supervised reconstruction objective. We demonstrate on both simulated and real-world datasets that the learned latent space is interpretable and controllable: by manipulating particle positions and decoding, we can generate novel scene configurations. Furthermore, we show that leveraging these compact 3D latent particles for downstream robotic manipulation improves performance over baselines that either lack explicit 3D information or rely on memory-intensive dense 3D inputs without object-centric structure. Code and videos are available at https://eubooks3003.github.io/3d-dlp.

</details>


### [118] [Can In-Context Learning Support Intrinsic Curiosity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19476)
*Eric Elmoznino,Sangnie Bhardwaj,Johannes von Oswald,Rajai Nasser,Blaise Agüera y Arcas,João Sacramento,Rif A. Saurous,Guillaume Lajoie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究能否利用序列模型的内在学习（ICL）能力替代传统梯度下降，实现无更新的学习进度估计以驱动探索。理论上证明在一般MDP中该方法存在偏差不可行，但在非时序场景（如主动学习）中ICL奖励能有效逼近真实学习进度，实验验证了该方法可训练出最优探索策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于"学习进度"的探索奖励需要昂贵的梯度下降内循环，计算成本高、难以规模化。作者希望利用序列模型的内在学习（ICL）能力作为无需更新的即时世界模型，消除这一计算瓶颈。

Method: 理论分析：证明在一般MDP中，基于ICL预测误差和反事实上下文操作无法无偏估计学习进度；非时序场景（主动学习/贝叶斯实验设计）中，ICL奖励可上界并渐近收敛到真实学习进度。实验验证：在连续和符号环境中训练好奇数据收集策略。

Result: 一般MDP中ICL方法存在偏差不可行；非时序场景中ICL奖励有效逼近真实学习进度；实验表明该方法可成功训练出最优探索策略。

Conclusion: ICL能够作为高效的学习进度估计器，但其适用范围受限于非时序场景；为设计可扩展的内在好奇心机制提供了理论边界和实用路径。

Abstract: Effective machine learning depends not only on how we model data, but also on what data we choose to collect. While large sequence models have revolutionized data modeling, the problem of automated data selection, or "intrinsic curiosity", remains a significant challenge. Classic approaches incentivize exploration by rewarding an agent based on its "learning progress", which measures how much a newly acquired observation improves a world model's predictive ability. However, evaluating these rewards traditionally requires expensive inner loops of gradient descent updates within each trajectory, rendering them computationally impractical at scale. In this work, we investigate whether the emergent in-context learning (ICL) capabilities of sequence models can eliminate this bottleneck by serving as immediate, update-free world models. Specifically, we evaluate whether an exploration policy can be trained to maximize learning progress, using solely the prediction errors and counterfactual context manipulations of an in-context learner. We first prove that in general Markov decision processes, this is in fact impossible in an unbiased way: the resulting intrinsic rewards either suffer from nuisance terms that bias their estimation of true learning progress, or they cannot be implemented using an in-context learner's prediction errors. Conversely, we prove a positive result for a broad subclass of non-temporal settings, encompassing active learning and Bayesian Experimental Design: here, ICL-derived rewards successfully bound and asymptotically converge to the true learning progress. We corroborate our theory with controlled experiments across continuous and symbolic environments, demonstrating that our ICL-driven framework successfully trains curious data-collection policies that explore optimally.

</details>


### [119] [Insulin4RL: Real-Time Insulin Management in the Intensive Care Unit for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19481)
*Thomas Frost,Steve Harris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Insulin4RL，一个基于MIMIC-IV构建的医疗离线强化学习数据集，包含超过37.5万个标签决策和12,209名ICU患者的真实临床轨迹，采用自然不规则的输入和动作时间间隔，旨在解决传统EHR数据离散化带来的泛化性问题，并提供了基线性能指标和标准化评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线强化学习在医疗决策中的训练和评估依赖固定时间间隔离散化的EHR数据，这种离散化创建了复杂临床场景的虚构表示，损害了回顾性模型评估的泛化性。作者因此需要一个具有自然不规则输入和动作的真实临床轨迹数据集。

Method: 从MIMIC-IV数据库中提取ICU中需要胰岛素输注剂量调整的患者数据，构建包含375,000+标签决策、12,209名患者的数据集，保留临床轨迹的自然不规则时间间隔特征，并采用无模型离线强化学习建立基线性能指标，使用拟合Q评估提供标准化评估协议。

Result: 成功构建了Insulin4RL数据集，提供了其结构和特征描述，给出了无模型离线强化学习的基线性能指标，并建立了使用拟合Q评估的标准化评估协议。

Conclusion: Insulin4RL可作为研究离线强化学习模型在真实临床采样假设下性能的资源，作者还提出了未来研究方向的建议。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (ORL) offers the potential to improve the quality of clinical decision-making using historical electronic health record (EHR) data. Current training and evaluative practices in this field rely heavily on EHR datasets that have been temporally discretised into fixed, regular time intervals. Discretisation creates fictional representations of complex clinical scenarios and compromises the generalisability of retrospective model evaluations. In this paper, we introduce Insulin4RL, a healthcare ORL dataset featuring naturally irregular inputs and actions from real clinical trajectories. Derived from MIMIC-IV, Insulin4RL comprises over 375,000 labelled decisions across 12,209 patients requiring insulin infusion titration in the Intensive Care Unit. The dataset can thus be used for research into ORL model performance under realistic clinical sampling assumptions. We provide a description of the dataset's structure and characteristics, baseline performance metrics using model-free offline reinforcement learning, and a standardised evaluation protocol using fitted Q-evaluation. We conclude with suggested areas for future research that could be addressed using this resource.

</details>


### [120] [Concept Flow Models: Anchoring Concept-Based Reasoning with Hierarchical Bottlenecks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19489)
*Ya Wang,Adrian Paschke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出概念流模型（CFM），用层级决策树替代扁平瓶颈层，通过逐层聚焦判别性子概念来缓解概念瓶颈模型中的信息泄漏问题，在保持预测性能的同时提升可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念瓶颈模型（CBM）在概念数量接近嵌入维度时存在严重的信息泄漏问题，模型会利用虚假或语义无关的相关性，损害可解释性。

Method: 提出概念流模型（CFM），将扁平瓶颈替换为层级化、概念驱动的决策树。每个内部节点聚焦于局部判别性子概念，逐步缩小预测范围。通过视觉嵌入构建决策层级，在各层级分配语义概念，并通过概率树遍历训练可微概念权重。

Result: 在多个基准上，CFM在保持与扁平CBM相当预测性能的同时，通过减少有效概念使用显著缓解了信息泄漏，并生成了逐步决策流。

Conclusion: 层级化概念结构能有效缓解信息泄漏问题，同时保持模型性能和可解释性，并支持透明可审计的推理过程。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) enhance interpretability by projecting learned features into a human-understandable concept space. Recent approaches leverage vision-language models to generate concept embeddings, reducing the need for manual concept annotations. However, these models suffer from a critical limitation: as the number of concepts approaches the embedding dimension, information leakage increases, enabling the model to exploit spurious or semantically irrelevant correlations and undermining interpretability. In this work, we propose Concept Flow Models (CFMs), which replace the flat bottleneck with a hierarchical, concept-driven decision tree. Each internal node in the hierarchy focuses on a localized subset of discriminative concepts, progressively narrowing the prediction scope. Our framework constructs decision hierarchies from visual embeddings, distributes semantic concepts at each hierarchy level, and trains differentiable concept weights through probabilistic tree traversal. Extensive experiments on diverse benchmarks demonstrate that CFMs match the predictive performance of flat CBMs, while substantially mitigating information leakage by reducing effective concept usage. Furthermore, CFMs yield stepwise decision flows that enable transparent and auditable model reasoning with hierarchical class structures.

</details>


### [121] [Algebraic Dead Directions in LayerNorm Transformers: A Forward-Pass-Only Diagnostic at LLM Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19491)
*Tejas Pradeep Shirodkar,P. J. Narayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现LayerNorm Transformer的逆尺度方向γ⁻¹/‖γ⁻¹‖是后最终归一化中心化激活协方差矩阵的精确代数核，可直接从LN尺度参数读出（无需前向/反向传播或特征分解）。在14个预训练Transformer上验证，该方向在初始化时与SVD底部奇异方向吻合至小数点后四位（9/9 LayerNorm模型），训练后特征值加深约10³倍。该方向的存在可用于分类归一化类型、检测残差流奇异结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练Transformer位于损失函数的近奇异最小值处，Fisher信息矩阵沿某些"死亡方向"退化。现有方法需要前向传播和特征分解或采样估计来定位这些方向，无法从网络参数本身直接计算。作者希望找到一种仅从参数即可读出的死亡方向。

Method: 理论分析LayerNorm中仿射变换的逆尺度方向γ⁻¹/‖γ⁻¹‖，证明其为后最终归一化中心化激活协方差矩阵的精确代数核，对任意输入分布成立。该方向仅需读取LN尺度参数，无需任何前向/反向传播或特征分解。在9个LayerNorm和5个RMSNorm模型上验证（160M-35B参数规模，语言和视觉任务）。

Result: 随机初始化时，预测方向与通过一次前向传播+SVD测量的底部奇异方向在9/9 LayerNorm模型上吻合至小数点后四位；在5/5 RMSNorm模型上正确缺失（因缺乏均值减法投影器）。训练后该方向的协方差特征值加深约10³倍，并出现更多死亡方向。残差流的最小奇异值在13/14个Transformer上保持逐块不变，唯一的例外（Gemma4-31B）被该方向精确定位。

Conclusion: 提出了迄今为止最廉价的死亡方向读取方法（仅针对LayerNorm），可通过参数直接分类归一化类型（LayerNorm vs RMSNorm），并能单次前向传播检测训练带来的奇异结构变化。

Abstract: Pretrained transformers sit near singular minima of the loss, where the Fisher information metric degenerates along dead directions: directions in parameter space along which the directional Fisher vanishes. Locating such a direction normally needs a forward pass and an eigendecomposition of activations, or a sampling-based complexity estimate; none returns a direction computable from the network's parameters alone. We give one, for LayerNorm transformers. The inverse-scale direction $γ^{-1}/\|γ^{-1}\|$ of the LayerNorm affine is an exact algebraic kernel of the post-final-norm centred activation covariance, for any input distribution, and induces a corresponding dead direction in parameter space. It is read from the LN scale parameter alone, with no forward or backward pass and no eigensolve: the cheapest dead-direction read, specific to LayerNorm. We test it on $14$ pretrained transformers ($9$ LayerNorm, $5$ RMSNorm; $160$M-$35$B; language and vision objectives). At random initialisation the predicted direction matches the measured bottom singular direction (one forward pass, direct SVD) to four decimal places on $9/9$ LayerNorm models, and is correctly absent on $5/5$ RMSNorm models, which lack the mean-subtraction projector that creates it. On the trained checkpoint the covariance eigenvalue along this direction deepens by ${\sim}10^3\times$ and further dead directions open; the random-init-to-trained gap is a one-forward-pass, per-checkpoint readout of singular structure along the predicted coordinate. Two consequences follow in closed form: the residual stream's smallest singular value is preserved block-to-block on $13/14$ transformers measured on their own input distribution, the one exception (Gemma$4$-$31$B) a genuine dead direction the same read pinpoints; and the kernel direction's presence classifies a transformer's normalisation from the parameters alone.

</details>


### [122] [Calibrating Generative Models to Feature Distributions with MMD Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19496)
*Nathaniel L. Diamant,Brian L. Trippe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出kCGM方法，通过最小化生成样本与目标集特征分布之间的最大均值差异（MMD），并使用KL正则化保持与预训练模型的接近，从而在不牺牲化学有效性的前提下校正生成模型的特征分布偏差。在抗生素分子、蛋白质和DNA生成任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型在分布层面上可能产生显著偏差：预训练模型虽能生成单个合理样本，但关键特征的分布与目标集（如已知抗生素）不匹配。直接微调会导致过拟合且无法控制特征匹配，需要一种能校正分布偏差而不牺牲有效性的方法。

Method: kCGM（kernel Calibrating Generative Models）：通过无偏得分函数估计器最小化生成样本与目标特征分布之间的MMD，并加入KL散度正则化项以保持与预训练模型的接近，从而在特征层面进行监督校正。

Result: 在174种抗生素的目标集上，直接微调牺牲了化学有效性来换取特征分布匹配，而kCGM在提升目标特征匹配的同时提高了化学有效性。在蛋白质和DNA生成任务中，kCGM成功适配了自回归、连续空间扩散和离散扩散等不同模型。

Conclusion: kCGM是一种通用的、仅需特征层级监督即可校正生成模型分布偏差的方法，适用于多种生成模型架构和生物序列生成任务，有望在药物发现等实际应用中发挥重要作用。

Abstract: Generative models can produce individually plausible samples while deviating substantially from a target set in the distribution of key features. For example, a model pretrained on broad drug-like chemical space may generate molecules whose molecular features differ from those of a therapeutic class of interest, such as known antibiotics. Correcting such distributional miscalibration is challenging: direct finetuning on the target set can overfit and does not control which features are matched. To fill this gap, we introduce kernel Calibrating Generative Models (kCGM). kCGM minimizes a maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) between generated and target feature distributions using an unbiased score-function estimator, with KL regularization to remain close to the pretrained model. On a target set of 174 antibiotics, direct finetuning sacrifices chemical validity for feature-distribution matching, whereas kCGM improves target feature matching while increasing validity. We further demonstrate kCGM in protein and DNA generation tasks, showing it can adapt autoregressive, continuous-space diffusion, and discrete diffusion models using only feature-level supervision. Code is available at https://github.com/smithhenryd/cgm.

</details>


### [123] [Interactive Pareto navigation for deep multi-task learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19521)
*Augustina C. Amakor,Konstantin Sonntag,Sebastian Peitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Preference Pareto Exploration (PPE)框架，通过预测-校正方法在帕累托流形上沿决策者偏好方向探索，利用Krylov子空间方法避免显式Hessian计算，实现高效的多目标深度学习优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务学习中，随着目标数量增加，加权聚合方法无法捕捉帕累托前沿的几何形状且计算成本高昂，难以反映决策者偏好。

Method: 提出PPE框架，基于预测-校正方法：预测步沿帕累托最优解流形的切空间向偏好方向移动，校正步回到帕累托前沿；利用Krylov子空间方法（仅需矩阵-向量乘积）避免显式Hessian计算，通过自动微分高效实现。

Result: 在玩具问题和深度学习实例上验证了方法的有效性和性能。

Conclusion: PPE通过交互式探索过程，在考虑帕累托集几何结构的同时强制执行决策者偏好，提升了多目标优化的效率和鲁棒性。

Abstract: In multi-task learning, handling an increasing number of objectives can quickly become challenging, both in terms of the computational resources and the decision maker's capacity to choose appropriate trade-offs. A widely used approach is thus to aggregate the individual losses in a single loss function by a weighted sum. This often fails to capture either the decision maker's preferences as a result of the shape of the Pareto front, or requires multiple adjustments and computations which becomes prohibitively expensive in deep learning applications. To address these issues, we introduce a novel framework, Preference Pareto Exploration (PPE), which enforces the decision maker's preferences while accounting for the geometry of the Pareto set in an interactive exploration process. PPE is based on a predictor-corrector method that performs predictor steps tangential to the manifold of Pareto-optimal solutions, following the decision maker's preference. The subsequent corrector step results in a new trade-off reflecting this preference. To avoid explicit Hessian computations when characterizing the tangent space of the manifold, we employ a Krylov subspace method that relies solely on matrix-vector products. These products can be efficiently obtained via automatic differentiation, ensuring both efficiency and robustness throughout the optimization process. The method's functionality and performance are demonstrated using both toy problems and examples from deep learning.

</details>


### [124] [Techniques for Peak Memory Reduction for LoRA Fine-tuning of LLMs on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19528)
*Hassan Dbouk,Matthias Reisser,Prathamesh Mandke,Likhita Arun Navali,Christos Louizos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套互补技术（量化、高效检查点、softmax近似、logits掩码），在消费级硬件上实现高达28倍峰值内存 reduction，使得数亿参数LLM的LoRA微调成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在消费级硬件上使用LoRA微调LLM面临严峻的内存瓶颈，峰值内存常超出设备限制，尤其是长上下文训练场景下，阻碍了隐私保护下的个性化模型定制。

Method: (1) 基模型量化与即时反量化；(2) 结合选择性激活缓存和磁盘卸载的高效检查点机制；(3) 利用语义相关token子集的softmax近似；(4) logits掩码。

Result: 在Llama-3.2 3B和Qwen-2.5 3B上分别实现26倍和28倍的峰值内存缩减，使微调能在资源受限设备上运行。

Conclusion: 所提技术在不牺牲模型质量的前提下显著降低内存占用，为消费级硬件上的大模型微调提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Fine-tuning of Large Language Models (LLMs) using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) on an end-user's data offers personalized experiences while keeping data private, but faces severe memory constraints on consumer hardware. Peak memory during fine-tuning often exceeds device limits, especially for models with billions of parameters and long-context training data. This paper introduces a suite of complementary techniques to reduce memory footprint without sacrificing model quality: (1) base model quantization with on-the-fly dequantization, (2) memory-efficient checkpointing combining selective activation caching and disk offloading, (3) softmax approximation using semantically relevant token subsets, and (4) logits masking. Experiments on Llama-3.2 3B and Qwen-2.5 3B demonstrate up to $26\times$ and $28\times$ reduction in peak memory, enabling fine-tuning on resource-constrained devices.

</details>


### [125] [Tracking Representation Dynamics in Large Language Models with Persistent Homology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19542)
*Naman Malhotra,Jay Ambadkar,Abhinav Gupta,Kushal Kasivel,Abbas Schwarz,Kamillo Ferry,Anthea Monod*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用持续同调（persistent homology）研究大语言模型在监督微调过程中的内部表征拓扑变化，发现大多数拓扑重组发生在训练早期，不同对齐目标诱导出可区分的拓扑轨迹，且指令微调与预训练模型表现出不同的演化模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通常通过监督微调进行对齐，但内部表征如何在此过程中演化尚不清楚。现有研究多关注行为指标，缺乏对表征层面变化的理解。

Method: 采用持续同调分析激活空间的拓扑结构，对4个1B-7B参数的Transformer语言模型，在helpful、harmless、混合三种对齐目标下进行微调，通过密集检查点分析拓扑活动。

Result: 大多数拓扑重组发生在训练早期；存在短暂的拓扑活动峰值后迅速稳定；不同对齐目标产生可区分的拓扑轨迹；指令微调与预训练模型呈现定性不同的演化模式。

Conclusion: 持续同调为对齐研究提供了互补视角，揭示了行为指标无法体现的表征层面变化。

Abstract: Large language models are commonly aligned through supervised fine-tuning, yet little is known about how their internal representations evolve during this process. We study alignment dynamics using persistent homology by tracking the topology of activation spaces throughout fine-tuning. Across four transformer language models ranging from 1B to 7B parameters and three alignment objectives corresponding to helpful, harmless, and mixed training data, we find that the majority of topological reorganization occurs during the earliest stages of training. A dense checkpoint analysis reveals a transient peak in topological activity followed by rapid stabilization. We further show that different alignment objectives induce distinguishable topological trajectories, while instruction-tuned and pretrained models exhibit qualitatively different patterns of evolution. Our results suggest that persistent homology provides a complementary perspective on alignment, revealing representation-level changes that are not apparent from behavioral metrics alone.

</details>


### [126] [Predicting Mergeability of Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19549)
*Lin Tang,Wei Zhang,Jing Li,Hongyu Chen,Ming Zhao,Yuxuan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MergeProbe，一种在LoRA适配器训练早期（前几个百分点）即可预测其合并后性能的轻量级方法，通过测量低秩更新与梯度的对齐程度以及共享表示扰动等信号，实现了比现有干扰感知合并方法更好的平均和最差保留性能，将LoRA合并从事后工程转变为预期性测量问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA适配器单独训练时表现良好，但合并后可能产生破坏性干扰，而这种干扰通常只有在两个适配器都完全训练和评估后才能被发现，反馈成本高昂。因此需要一种能够在训练早期就预测适配器合并可行性的方法。

Method: 将适配器可合并性形式化为合并后保留单任务效用的程度，并从训练早期（前几个百分点）的信号中预测：主要包括低秩更新与梯度在任务间的对齐程度，以及对共享表示的扰动大小。将这些信号打包为MergeProbe，一种轻量级预测器，可估计成对和集合级的保留性能，并做出具体决策：直接合并、重加权、剪枝或路由。

Result: 在MERGE-PEFT基准（涵盖数学、代码、科学、指令遵循和安全五个领域）上，MergeProbe在平均和最差保留性能上均优于强干扰感知合并基线，并且部署开销远低于完整的任务路由方法。

Conclusion: 将LoRA合并从事后的工程步骤转变为一个预期性的测量问题。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) makes it cheap to train many domain- and task-specific language model adapters, but whether two adapters can be merged is usually discovered only after both have been fully trained and evaluated. This late feedback is costly: adapters that are strong in isolation can interfere destructively once their updates are combined. We ask whether this outcome can be anticipated. We formalize adapter mergeability as the degree to which an adapter preserves its single-task utility after merging, and show that it can be forecast from signals measured in the first few percent of training -- chiefly how the low-rank updates and their gradients align across tasks and how much they disturb shared representations. We package these signals into MergeProbe, a lightweight predictor that estimates pairwise and set-level retention and turns the estimate into a concrete decision: merge directly, reweight, prune, or route. On MERGE-PEFT, a five-domain benchmark spanning math, code, science, instruction following, and safety, MergeProbe attains the best average and worst-case retention among strong interference-aware merge baselines while adding far less deployment overhead than full task routing. This turns LoRA merging from a post-hoc engineering step into an anticipatory measurement problem.

</details>


### [127] [Displacement Is Not Direction: Evaluating Fidelity Metrics for Quantized LLM Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19558)
*Miloš Nikolić,Ali Hadi Zadeh,Enrique Torres Sanchez,Andreas Moshovos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了以KL散度（KLD）为代表的保真度指标作为基准质量代理指标的有效性。研究发现，虽然KLD在全量化范围内与基准得分强相关，但在接近基线的“静默区间”中这种相关性消失。进一步分析表明，KLD主要衡量与参考模型的分歧量而非分歧方向，导致其性能预测能力和跨模型路由能力均很弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中常用KL散度等保真度指标作为高质量基准测试的低成本代理，但这种做法的实际有效性缺乏系统性验证。

Method: 基于Qwen3.6-35B-A3B的28个量化版本和Devstral-Small-2-24B的41个量化版本，在下游基准测试套件上进行评估，分析KLD与基准分数的相关性，并从分歧量和分歧方向两个维度进行结构分解分析。

Result: KLD在全量化范围内与基准分数强相关（Qwen: ρ=-0.72, Devstral: ρ=-0.86, p<0.001），但在近基线静默区间内相关性不显著（Qwen: ρ=+0.00, Devstral: ρ=-0.24, p=0.36）。该现象在14种测量变体中均存在。单提示级别上，KLD仅在代码任务上有弱故障预测能力（失败/成功几何均值比[1.08,1.22]），作为跨模型路由器的准确率仅42.3%-49.4%。结构分解显示，KLD主要测量与参考模型的分歧量（静默区间复合ρ分别达+0.94和+0.55），而与分歧方向的关系很弱且依赖于任务。

Conclusion: KL散度作为基准质量代理指标存在根本性局限：它主要捕捉分歧量而非分歧方向，因此在区分不同错误类型和预测模型性能方面能力有限，不宜作为通用代理指标使用。

Abstract: Fidelity metrics, such as per-token KL divergence (KLD) against a high-precision reference, are often used in practice as low-cost proxies for benchmark quality. We test this practice on a 28-quant cohort of Qwen3.6-35B-A3B and a 41-quant cohort of Devstral-Small-2-24B, evaluated across a suite of downstream benchmarks. We find that KLD is strongly correlated with benchmark score over the full cohort ($ρ=-0.72$ on Qwen and $ρ=-0.86$ on Devstral, both with $p<0.001$). However, this relationship collapses to non-significance in the near-baseline silent zone ($ρ=+0.00$ on Qwen and $ρ=-0.24$, $p=0.36$, on Devstral). This collapse persists across 14 measurement variants, including different KLD aggregations, perplexity formulations, top-1 agreement, calibration corpora, and context lengths. At the per-prompt level, KLD has only weak failure-prediction power on code, with failed-vs-passed geometric-mean ratios in $[1.08,1.22]$ across five models on LiveCodeBench, and fails as a cross-model router, achieving only $42.3\%-49.4\%$ accuracy on disagreement prompts. We trace the collapse to a structural decomposition: KLD primarily measures the volume of disagreement with the reference, with silent-zone composite $ρ=+0.94$ ($p<0.001$) on Qwen and $+0.55$ ($p=0.03$) on Devstral, while its relationship to the direction of those disagreements is weak and task-conditional.

</details>


### [128] [Understanding Key Features of Time Series Foundation Models from Epidemic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19560)
*Alireza Jafari,Judy Fox,Geoffrey C. Fox,Madhav Marathe,Aniruddha Adiga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了多种现代时间序列预测架构（经典神经网络、数值Transformer、预训练基础模型、LLM方法）在美国区域性流感预测中的表现，发现融合多种预训练预测器的混合专家模型性能最优，数值Transformer表现可靠，预训练在长周期预测中收益最大，而LLM方法表现不佳。住院数据作为辅助信号可提升特定场景下的预测鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 季节性流感每年在美国造成大量发病和死亡，准确的短期预测对疫苗接种时机、医院人员调配和资源配置至关重要，而现代预测架构在传染病监测数据上的比较行为尚未得到充分刻画。

Method: 基于流感样疾病监测和流感相关住院时间序列，在时间和空间泛化设置下进行1-4周前瞻预测，比较了经典神经网络、数值Transformer、预训练时间序列基础模型和基于LLM的预测方法。

Result: 混合专家模型（融合多种预训练预测器）取得最优性能；数值Transformer预测可靠；预训练在长周期（尤其是当预训练域与流感动力学机制对齐时）收益最大；LLM方法表现不及数值预测器；住院信号作为辅助协变量和预训练来源可在特定场景下提升多周期预测的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 研究为流感防控中的模型选择、预训练策略和辅助信号使用提供了可操作的指导建议。

Abstract: Seasonal influenza infects millions of people and causes substantial morbidity and mortality in the United States each year, making accurate short-term forecasting a core public-health need. Reliable forecasts of epidemic time series can inform vaccination timing, hospital staffing, and resource allocation, yet the comparative behavior of modern forecasting architectures on infectious-disease surveillance data remains insufficiently characterized. We address this gap through a systematic evaluation of regional influenza forecasting using influenza-like illness surveillance and influenza-associated hospitalization time series under both temporal and spatial generalization settings for 1-4-week-ahead prediction. We compare classical neural network architectures, numerical transformer-based models, pretrained time series foundation models, and LLM-based forecasting approaches. Across tasks, we demonstrate that a mixture-of-experts model that fuses multiple pretrained forecasters achieves the strongest overall performance, indicating that heterogeneous pretrained representations provide complementary predictive information. Our results further show that numerical transformer-based models produce reliable forecasts, while pretraining provides the largest gains at longer horizons, particularly when the pretraining domain is mechanistically aligned with influenza dynamics. In contrast, LLM-based time series methods underperform relative to numerical forecasters in this setting. Finally, we examine hospitalization information as both an auxiliary covariate and a pretraining source. Hospitalization signals provide complementary improvements in selected settings and clarify when additional surveillance streams enhance the robustness of multi-horizon forecasting. These findings provide actionable guidance on model selection, pretraining strategy, and auxiliary-signal use for influenza preparedness.

</details>


### [129] [Advances in Scientific Machine Learning for Coupled Fluid Flow and Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19562)
*Gabriel F. Barros,Rômulo M. Silva,Alvaro L. G. A. Coutinho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了科学机器学习（SciML）在不可压Navier-Stokes与标量输运耦合流动建模中的最新进展，重点介绍了基于SVD的线性降阶方法（如DMD）、物理信息神经网络（PINNs）和β-VAE等非线性方法，并结合高性能计算策略（自适应网格加密/粗化、浮点数据压缩）构建高效替代模型。文中还提出了两个新贡献：利用PINNs对浊流进行代理建模，以及利用β-VAE从热对流中提取解缠结非线性模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合流体流动与输运现象（如浊流、热对流）具有强非线性和多尺度特性，高保真数值模拟计算成本极高，亟需开发能够大幅降低计算代价同时保持足够精度的替代模型。

Method: 综述了SciML方法，包括线性降阶技术（基于SVD的DMD）和非线性神经网络方法（PINNs、β-VAEs），并与HPC策略（AMR/C、数据压缩）相结合。新贡献包括：PINNs用于浊流代理建模，β-VAEs用于热对流中解缠结非线性模态提取。

Result: 在特定数据范围和建模假设下，SciML方法能够快速精确地近似复杂耦合系统，显著降低全阶模拟的计算成本。PINNs成功用于浊流代理建模，β-VAEs实现了从热对流中提取解缠结的非线性模态。

Conclusion: SciML为复杂耦合流动系统提供了高效准确的替代建模方案，显著降低了计算成本。实时预测和不确定性量化等更广泛的能力仍是活跃的研究方向，其可行性高度依赖于具体问题。

Abstract: This chapter reviews recent advances in Scientific Machine Learning (SciML) for modeling coupled fluid flow and transport phenomena governed by the incompressible Navier-Stokes and scalar transport equations. Such systems, found in applications like turbidity currents and thermal convection, feature strong nonlinear coupling and multiscale behavior that make high-fidelity simulations computationally expensive. To address this, the chapter surveys state-of-the-art SciML methods for building efficient surrogate models, including linear reduced-order techniques based on Singular Value Decomposition (such as Dynamic Mode Decomposition) and nonlinear neural network approaches like Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) and $β$-Variational Autoencoders ($β$-VAEs). It first covers the authors' work combining these models with High Performance Computing strategies, including Adaptive Mesh Refinement/Coarsening (AMR/C) and scientific floating-point data compression. It then presents two new contributions: surrogate modeling of turbidity currents via PINNs, and the extraction of disentangled nonlinear modes from thermal flows using $β$-VAEs. Governing equations and representative benchmarks, including lock-exchange flows and Rayleigh-Bénard convection, illustrate these methodologies. The chapter is intentionally long, covering both the mathematical and physical foundations of coupled fluid flow and the computational aspects of state-of-the-art modeling. Overall, it demonstrates how SciML enables fast, accurate approximations of complex coupled systems within the specific data regimes and modeling assumptions considered, while substantially reducing computational cost relative to full-order simulations. Broader capabilities such as real-time prediction and uncertainty quantification remain active research directions whose feasibility depends strongly on the problem at hand.

</details>


### [130] [On the QUEST for Uncertainty Quantification via Highest Density Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19569)
*Sam Goring,Tom Kuipers,Nicola Paoletti,David S. Watson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QUEST框架，基于最高密度区域的体积来量化不确定性，克服了传统基于逐点预测风险的方法在非条件期望目标下的反直觉问题，并通过公理化验证和实验证明了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于适当评分规则的不确定性量化方法在目标统计量非条件期望时会产生反直觉结果，缺乏满足公理化性质的不确定性度量框架。

Method: 提出QUEST方法，基于分布支撑集上最高密度区域的Lebesgue测度浓度来刻画不确定性，引入鲁棒参数α，并连接信息论和经济学中的经典统计量。

Result: QUEST的认知不确定性和偶然不确定性度量满足一组公理（包括分布扩散下的单调性和位置偏移不变性），在选择性预测基准上优于方差和微分熵等标准度量。

Conclusion: QUEST为概率机器学习中的不确定性量化提供了一种新的公理化框架，在理论上优于基于适当评分规则的传统方法，并在实验中表现出色。
